It wasn’t like one clean block the whole time, but the cycling between feeling charged up and then crashing has been going on about a month.\n\nDoctor: Did any one of those energized, lit-up periods last at least 4 days in a row?\n\nPatient: I’m not totally sure if I can separate it out cleanly as exactly four days. The really obvious cycling between feeling charged up and then crashing has been going on about a month now, since the mortgage stuff became real.\n\nDoctor: During those energized periods, were you more active or taking on more projects than usual?\n\nPatient: Yes. What I know is the really obvious back-and-forth between feeling charged up and then crashing has been going on about a month, since the mortgage became real.\n\nDoctor: During those energized times, did other people clearly notice that you were acting differently than your usual self?\n\nPatient: Yeah, my sister noticed.
